	<div class="row">

		
		<div class="fl-content fl-content-left col-md-10">
			<article class="fl-post post-3785 post type-post status-publish format-standard has-post-thumbnail hentry category-artist" id="fl-post-3785" itemscope itemtype="https://schema.org/BlogPosting">

	
	<header class="fl-post-header">
		<h1 class="fl-post-title" itemprop="headline">
			Nakamura, Joel					</h1>
		<meta itemscope itemprop="mainEntityOfPage" itemtype="https://schema.org/WebPage" itemid="https://www.newmexicopbs.org/productions/colores/nakamura-joel/" content="Nakamura, Joel" /><meta itemprop="datePublished" content="2017-10-16" /><meta itemprop="dateModified" content="2021-05-18" /><div itemprop="publisher" itemscope itemtype="https://schema.org/Organization"><meta itemprop="name" content="¡COLORES!"><div itemprop="logo" itemscope itemtype="https://schema.org/ImageObject"><meta itemprop="url" content="https://www.newmexicopbs.org/productions/colores/wp-content/uploads/2021/06/blue_nmpbs_logo_NO_TM_MEDIUM.png"></div></div><div itemscope itemprop="author" itemtype="https://schema.org/Person"><meta itemprop="url" content="https://www.newmexicopbs.org/productions/colores/author/web-czar/" /><meta itemprop="name" content="Web Czar" /></div><div itemscope itemprop="image" itemtype="https://schema.org/ImageObject"><meta itemprop="url" content="https://www.newmexicopbs.org/productions/colores/wp-content/uploads/2016/10/joel-nakamura.jpg" /><meta itemprop="width" content="1280" /><meta itemprop="height" content="720" /></div><div itemprop="interactionStatistic" itemscope itemtype="https://schema.org/InteractionCounter"><meta itemprop="interactionType" content="https://schema.org/CommentAction" /><meta itemprop="userInteractionCount" content="0" /></div>	</header><!-- .fl-post-header -->

	
	
	<div class="fl-post-content clearfix" itemprop="text">
		
<figure class="wp-block-image is-resized"><img fetchpriority="high" decoding="async" src="https://www.newmexicopbs.org/productions/colores/wp-content/uploads/2016/10/joel-nakamura-1024x576.jpg" alt="Joel Nakamura" class="wp-image-2917" width="512" height="288" srcset="https://www.newmexicopbs.org/productions/colores/wp-content/uploads/2016/10/joel-nakamura-1024x576.jpg 1024w, https://www.newmexicopbs.org/productions/colores/wp-content/uploads/2016/10/joel-nakamura-300x169.jpg 300w, https://www.newmexicopbs.org/productions/colores/wp-content/uploads/2016/10/joel-nakamura-768x432.jpg 768w, https://www.newmexicopbs.org/productions/colores/wp-content/uploads/2016/10/joel-nakamura.jpg 1280w" sizes="(max-width: 512px) 100vw, 512px" /></figure>



<div style="height:20px" aria-hidden="true" class="wp-block-spacer"></div>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Born sometime between the Korean War and the Bay of Pigs in the City of Los Angeles, <strong>Joel Nakamura</strong> is a graduate of the world-renowned Art Center College of Design in Pasadena where he later became an instructor. He, his wife Kathleen and their two children live about 10 minutes south of Santa Fe Plaza. His work has been featured in newspapers and magazines across the United States. He has been the subject of numerous one-man shows, with his home gallery Hahn-Ross on Canyon Road in Santa Fe. He counts today two young persons – his children – as the sources for some of his most compelling and intriguing ideas in paint.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">“They are my greatest work of art, my greatest inspiration, in life,” he says.<br></p>


<p>Episodes:<br /><a href="https://www.newmexicopbs.org/productions/colores/artist-sasha-vom-dorp-artist-nanibah-chacon-painter-joel-nakamura/" aria-label="“Artist Sasha Vom Dorp, artist Nanibah Chacon, painter Joel Nakamura” (Edit)">Artist Sasha Vom Dorp, artist Nanibah Chacon, painter Joel Nakamura</a><br /><a class="row-title" href="https://www.newmexicopbs.org/productions/colores/october-8-2016/" aria-label="“Joel Nakamura, Robert Miley, Ralph Fasanella, Carole Harris” (Edit)">Joel Nakamura, Robert Miley, Ralph Fasanella, Carole Harris</a></p>
<p><a href="http://www.joelnakamura.com/about/">Website</a></p>


<p class="wp-block-paragraph"></p>
	</div><!-- .fl-post-content -->

	
			
</article>
